let currentGrille = null; let activeTab = {}; const answers = {}; const comments = {}; function getItems(code) { const g = GRILLES[code]; if (g.tabs) { const result = {}; g.tabs.forEach(function(tab) { result[tab.code] = tab.sections.flatMap(function(s) { return s.items; }); }); return result; } return { [code]: g.sections.flatMap(function(s) { return s.items; }) }; } function initAnswers(code) { const g = GRILLES[code]; const codes = g.tabs ? g.tabs.map(function(t) { return t.code; }) : [code]; const sectionSets = g.tabs ? g.tabs.map(function(t) { return t.sections; }) : [g.sections]; codes.forEach(function(c, i) { if (!answers[c]) { answers[c] = {}; comments[c] = {}; let idx = 0; sectionSets[i].forEach(function(s) { s.items.forEach(function() { answers[c][idx] = 'OK'; comments[c][idx] = ''; idx++; }); }); } }); } function setAnswer(tabCode, idx, val) { answers[tabCode][idx] = val; const row = document.querySelector('[data-tab="' + tabCode + '"][data-idx="' + idx + '"]'); if (!row) return; row.className = 'criteria-row' + (val === 'KO' ? ' is-ko' : val === 'NA' ? ' is-na' : ''); row.querySelectorAll('.okna-btn').forEach(function(b) { b.classList.remove('selected'); }); row.querySelector('.okna-btn.' + val.toLowerCase()).classList.add('selected'); updateStats(currentGrille); } function updateStats(code) { const g = GRILLES[code]; let ok = 0, ko = 0, na = 0, total = 0, scoreMax = 0, scoreOK = 0; const tabs = g.tabs ? g.tabs : [{code: code, sections: g.sections}]; tabs.forEach(function(tab) { if (!answers[tab.code]) return; let idx = 0; tab.sections.forEach(function(s) { s.items.forEach(function(item) { const v = answers[tab.code][idx] || 'OK'; total++; if (v === 'OK') ok++; else if (v === 'KO') ko++; else na++; if (v !== 'NA' && item.poids > 0) scoreMax += item.poids; if (v === 'OK' && item.poids > 0) scoreOK += item.poids; idx++; }); }); }); const taux = scoreMax > 0 ? Math.round(scoreOK / scoreMax * 100) : 0; const el = document.getElementById('stats-' + code); if (el) el.innerHTML = 'OK: ' + ok + '' + 'KO: ' + ko + '' + 'NA: ' + na + '' + '' + total + ' critères' + 'Taux: ' + taux + '%'; } function setSectionNA(tabCode, sectionIdx) { var g = null; Object.values(GRILLES).forEach(function(gr) { if (gr.tabs) { gr.tabs.forEach(function(t) { if (t.code === tabCode) g = gr; }); } else if (gr.code === tabCode) g = gr; }); if (!g) return; var sections = g.tabs ? g.tabs.find(function(t) { return t.code === tabCode; }).sections : g.sections; var startIdx = 0; for (var s = 0; s < sectionIdx; s++) startIdx += sections[s].items.length; var count = sections[sectionIdx].items.length; if (!answers[tabCode]) answers[tabCode] = {}; for (var i = startIdx; i < startIdx + count; i++) { answers[tabCode][i] = 'NA'; } renderGrille(currentGrille); } function buildSectionsHtml(tabCode, sections) { let html = ''; let globalIdx = 0; sections.forEach(function(section) { var sIdx = sections.indexOf(section); html += '
'; html += '
'; html += '' + section.title + ''; html += ''; html += '
'; section.items.forEach(function(item) { const idx = globalIdx; const val = (answers[tabCode] && answers[tabCode][idx]) ? answers[tabCode][idx] : 'OK'; const rowClass = 'criteria-row' + (val === 'KO' ? ' is-ko' : val === 'NA' ? ' is-na' : ''); html += '
'; html += '
' + item.text; if (item.hint) html += '
' + item.hint + '
'; html += ''; html += '
'; html += '' + (item.poids > 0 ? item.poids : '') + ''; html += '
'; ['OK','KO','NA'].forEach(function(v) { html += ''; }); html += '
'; globalIdx++; }); html += '
'; }); return html; } function renderGrille(code) { initAnswers(code); const g = GRILLES[code]; let html = '
'; html += '
'; if (g.tabs) { if (!activeTab[code]) activeTab[code] = g.tabs[0].code; const curTab = activeTab[code]; // Tab bar html += '
'; g.tabs.forEach(function(tab) { html += ''; }); html += '
'; // Active tab content const tab = g.tabs.find(function(t) { return t.code === curTab; }); html += buildSectionsHtml(tab.code, tab.sections); } else { html += buildSectionsHtml(code, g.sections); } document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = html; document.querySelectorAll('.comment-input').forEach(function(el) { el.addEventListener('change', function() { if (!comments[this.dataset.tab]) comments[this.dataset.tab] = {}; comments[this.dataset.tab][parseInt(this.dataset.idx)] = this.value; }); }); updateStats(code); } function switchTab(code, tabCode) { activeTab[code] = tabCode; renderGrille(code); } function resetGrille(code) { const g = GRILLES[code]; const tabs = g.tabs ? g.tabs : [{code: code, sections: g.sections}]; tabs.forEach(function(tab) { let idx = 0; tab.sections.forEach(function(s) { s.items.forEach(function() { answers[tab.code][idx] = 'OK'; comments[tab.code][idx] = ''; idx++; }); }); }); renderGrille(code); } function openGrille(code) { currentGrille = code; document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(function(b) { b.classList.remove('active'); }); document.getElementById('nav-' + code).classList.add('active'); document.getElementById('topbar-title').textContent = GRILLES[code].title; document.getElementById('btn-export').disabled = false; renderGrille(code); } function showList() { currentGrille = null; document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(function(b) { b.classList.remove('active'); }); document.getElementById('nav-list').classList.add('active'); document.getElementById('topbar-title').textContent = 'Toutes les grilles disponibles'; document.getElementById('btn-export').disabled = true; let html = '
'; Object.values(GRILLES).forEach(function(g) { let count; if (g.tabs) { count = g.tabs.reduce(function(a, tab) { return a + tab.sections.reduce(function(b, s) { return b + s.items.length; }, 0); }, 0); } else { count = g.sections.reduce(function(a, s) { return a + s.items.length; }, 0); } html += '
'; html += '' + g.code + ''; html += '
' + g.title + '
' + count + ' critères
'; html += '→
'; }); html += '
'; document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = html; } function exportXlsx() { if (!currentGrille) return; const nom = document.getElementById('input-nom').value.trim() || 'Prénom Nom'; const date = document.getElementById('input-date').value || today; const boa = document.getElementById('input-boa').value.trim() || ''; const code = currentGrille; const mapping = MAPPINGS[code]; function colLetterToNum(col) { let n = 0; for (let i = 0; i < col.length; i++) n = n * 26 + col.charCodeAt(i) - 64; return n - 1; } function parseCoord(coord) { const m = coord.match(/^([A-Z]+)(\d+)$/); return { c: colLetterToNum(m[1]), r: parseInt(m[2]) - 1 }; } function hexToArgb(hex) { return 'FF' + hex.toUpperCase(); } function buildSheet(sheetCode, tabCode, sheetData) { const styleData = CELL_STYLES[sheetCode]; const ws = {}; const usedCells = new Set(); // Apply all styled cells first (preserves structure) Object.entries(styleData.styles).forEach(function([coord, style]) { const pos = parseCoord(coord); const addr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell(pos); usedCells.add(addr); const cell = { t: 's', v: '' }; // Populate value from original template data const cellData = CELL_STYLES[sheetCode].styles[coord]; if (cellData && cellData.v !== undefined) { cell.v = cellData.v; cell.t = typeof cellData.v === 'number' ? 'n' : 's'; } cell.s = {}; if (style.bg) { cell.s.fill = { fgColor: { rgb: hexToArgb(style.bg) } }; } if (style.b || style.i) { cell.s.font = {}; if (style.b) cell.s.font.bold = true; if (style.i) cell.s.font.italic = true; } if (style.border) { const thin = { style: 'thin', color: { rgb: 'FF000000' } }; cell.s.border = { top: thin, bottom: thin, left: thin, right: thin }; } if (style.wrap) { cell.s.alignment = { wrapText: true }; } ws[addr] = cell; }); // Now inject values from original data (dossier, boa, criteria) // We need to repopulate values from the original cells info const origCells = CELL_STYLES[sheetCode].styles; // Set dossier/boa if (sheetData.dossierCell !== null) { const addr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell(sheetData.dossierCell); if (!ws[addr]) ws[addr] = { s: {} }; ws[addr].v = 'Dossier : ' + nom; ws[addr].t = 's'; if (!ws[addr].s.font) ws[addr].s.font = {}; ws[addr].s.font.bold = true; } if (sheetData.boaCell !== null) { const addr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell(sheetData.boaCell); if (!ws[addr]) ws[addr] = { s: {} }; ws[addr].v = 'BOA : ' + boa; ws[addr].t = 's'; if (!ws[addr].s.font) ws[addr].s.font = {}; ws[addr].s.font.bold = true; } // Inject OK/KO/NA values sheetData.criteriaRows.forEach(function(rowIdx, i) { const val = (answers[tabCode] && answers[tabCode][i] !== undefined) ? answers[tabCode][i] : 'OK'; const comment = (comments[tabCode] && comments[tabCode][i]) ? comments[tabCode][i] : ''; const okAddr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r: rowIdx, c: sheetData.okkoCol }); if (!ws[okAddr]) ws[okAddr] = { s: {} }; ws[okAddr].v = val; ws[okAddr].t = 's'; // Color the OK/KO/NA cell const bgColor = val === 'OK' ? 'EAF1ED' : val === 'KO' ? 'F7EDED' : 'EDEBE7'; ws[okAddr].s.fill = { fgColor: { rgb: 'FF' + bgColor } }; const thin = { style: 'thin', color: { rgb: 'FF000000' } }; ws[okAddr].s.border = { top: thin, bottom: thin, left: thin, right: thin }; if (comment) { const cmtAddr = XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r: rowIdx, c: sheetData.commentCol }); if (!ws[cmtAddr]) ws[cmtAddr] = { s: {} }; ws[cmtAddr].v = comment; ws[cmtAddr].t = 's'; } }); // Set ref range const allAddrs = Object.keys(ws).filter(k => k !== '!ref' && k !== '!merges' && k !== '!cols'); if (allAddrs.length > 0) { const decoded = allAddrs.map(a => XLSX.utils.decode_cell(a)); const minR = Math.min(...decoded.map(d => d.r)); const maxR = Math.max(...decoded.map(d => d.r)); const minC = Math.min(...decoded.map(d => d.c)); const maxC = Math.max(...decoded.map(d => d.c)); ws['!ref'] = XLSX.utils.encode_range({ s: { r: minR, c: minC }, e: { r: maxR, c: maxC } }); } // Merges if (styleData.merges && styleData.merges.length > 0) { ws['!merges'] = styleData.merges.map(function(m) { return XLSX.utils.decode_range(m); }); } // Column widths (convert from Excel units to chars) if (styleData.colWidths) { const cols = []; let maxCol = 0; Object.keys(styleData.colWidths).forEach(function(col) { const idx = colLetterToNum(col); if (idx > maxCol) maxCol = idx; }); for (let i = 0; i <= maxCol; i++) cols.push({ wch: 8 }); Object.entries(styleData.colWidths).forEach(function([col, w]) { cols[colLetterToNum(col)] = { wch: Math.round(w * 0.9) }; }); ws['!cols'] = cols; } return ws; } const wb = { SheetNames: [], Sheets: {}, Props: {} }; if (mapping.combined) { mapping.tabs.forEach(function(tabMapping) { const ws = buildSheet(tabMapping.code, tabMapping.code, tabMapping); wb.SheetNames.push(tabMapping.sheet); wb.Sheets[tabMapping.sheet] = ws; }); } else { const ws = buildSheet(code, code, mapping); wb.SheetNames.push(mapping.sheet); wb.Sheets[mapping.sheet] = ws; } const displayCode = code === 'A7A8' ? 'A7-A8' : code; XLSX.writeFile(wb, date + ' - GQualité ' + displayCode + ' - ' + nom + '.xlsx', { cellStyles: true }); } showList(); 7-A8' : code; XLSX.writeFile(wb, date + ' - GQualité ' + displayCode + ' - ' + nom + '.xlsx', { cellStyles: true }); } showList();
